# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Round 3 (2026-09-25): launch -> Home -> feed and the share-link open path.
# The rules above only cover the offline player fixture (app init + player initializer): nothing
# for Splash, the Browse shell, the feed presenters, InnerTube parsing or Glide. The embedded
# profile shipped ~2.6k rules, 87 of them com.newtube.mobile.
# (Update-day opens run uncompiled until background dexopt; see the art-profile task in build.gradle.)
#
# 1) Wildcards over the first-party code: HSP (startup) for the launch -> Home path, HP for the
#    rest (player, watch page, channel/search, parsing), plus Glide (every feed card) and the
#    Material widgets Home inflates. Class (L) rules for the startup set feed the app image.
# 2) Exact rules for every non-platform method an ART sampling trace of a cold share-link open on
#    the Pixel 9 observed (am start --start-profiler --sampling 1000; round-3 nt1.trace): the
#    library code on that path (OkHttp, Gson/JsonPath, brotli, media3, Cronet, Rx, WorkManager).
# Resolved against the 1.9.x release dex: ~35k methods, ~1.1k classes.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Launch -> Home (startup-critical): Splash, the Browse shell, its presenter and data, prefs, helpers.
